data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/SolarSystemCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\nimport { PlanetData, null) => void;\n}\n\ninterface PlanetMeshInfo {\n  id: string;\n  data: PlanetData;\n  pivot: THREE.Group;\n  mesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  currentOrbitAngle: number;\n  orbitDistance: number;\n  orbitSpeed: THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0));\n  const pointerDownPosRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n\n  // settingsの最新状態をアニメーションループ内で参照\n  const settingsRef = useRef<SimulationSettings>(settings);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n    // 軌道線表示切替\n    orbitLinesRef.current.forEach((line) => {\n      line.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n    });\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!container) return;\n\n    const width = container.clientWidth;\n    const height = container.clientHeight;\n\n    // 1. シーン初期化\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    sceneRef.current = scene;\n\n    // 2. カメラ初期化\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, width / height, 0.1, 2000);\n    cameraRef.current = camera;\n\n    // 3. レンダラー初期化\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });\n    renderer.setSize(width, height);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\n    container.innerHTML = '';\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n    rendererRef.current = renderer;\n\n    // 4. 背景の星屑パーティクル\n    const starsCount = 2000;\n    const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    const starPositions = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n    const starColors = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < starsCount; i++) {\n      const radius = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(300, 900);\n      const theta = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0, Math.PI * 2);\n      const phi = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0, Math.PI);\n\n      starPositions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n\n      const tint = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0.7, 1.0);\n      starColors[i * 3] = tint;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = tint * 0.95;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = tint * 1.1;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      size: 1.5,\n      vertexColors: true,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.85,\n    });\n    const starField = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);\n    scene.add(starField);\n\n    // 5. 環境光と光源（太陽光）\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.22);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff7ed, 3.2, 500, 0.5);\n    sunLight.position.set(0, 0, 0);\n    scene.add(sunLight);\n\n    // 6. 太陽の生成\n    const sunGeom = new THREE.SphereGeometry(SUN_DATA.radius, 32, 32);\n    const sunMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n      color: SUN_DATA.color,\n    });\n    const sunMesh = new THREE.Mesh(sunGeom, sunMat);\n    sunMesh.userData = { id: SUN_DATA.id };\n    scene.add(sunMesh);\n    sunMeshRef.current = sunMesh;\n\n    // 太陽の光冠グローメッシュ\n    const coronaGeom = new THREE.SphereGeometry(SUN_DATA.radius * 1.25, 32, 32);\n    const coronaMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n      color: 0xff6600,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.25,\n      side: THREE.BackSide,\n    });\n    const coronaMesh = new THREE.Mesh(coronaGeom, coronaMat);\n    sunMesh.add(coronaMesh);\n\n    // 7. 惑星および軌道線の生成\n    const planetsList: PlanetMeshInfo[] = [];\n    const orbitLinesList: THREE.Line[] = [];\n\n    PLANETS_DATA.forEach((pData, index) => {\n      // 軌道グループピボット\n      const orbitPivot = new THREE.Group();\n      scene.add(orbitPivot);\n\n      // 惑星本体\n      const pGeom = new THREE.SphereGeometry(pData.radius, 32, 32);\n      const pMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n        color: pData.color,\n        roughness: 0.6,\n        metalness: 0.2,\n      });\n      const pMesh = new THREE.Mesh(pGeom, pMat);\n      pMesh.userData = { id: pData.id };\n\n      // 初期の公転位置を散らして自然に見せる\n      const initialAngle = (index / PLANETS_DATA.length) * Math.PI * 2;\n      pMesh.position.set(\n        Math.cos(initialAngle) * pData.distance,\n        0,\n        Math.sin(initialAngle) * pData.distance\n      );\n      orbitPivot.add(pMesh);\n\n      // 土星などの環（リング）\n      let ringMesh: THREE.Mesh | undefined;\n      if (pData.rings) {\n        const ringGeom = new THREE.RingGeometry(pData.rings.innerRadius, pData.rings.outerRadius, 64);\n        const ringMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: pData.rings.color,\n          side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.8,\n          roughness: 0.5,\n        });\n        ringMesh = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeom, ringMat);\n        ringMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2 + 0.35; // 傾斜\n        pMesh.add(ringMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 軌道線（輪郭）\n      const curve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(\n        0, 0,\n        pData.distance, pData.distance,\n        0, 2 * Math.PI,\n        false,\n        0\n      );\n      const points = curve.getPoints(128);\n      const orbitLineGeom = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(\n        points.map((pt) => new THREE.Vector3(pt.x, 0, pt.y))\n      );\n      const orbitLineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n        color: 0x38bdf8,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.3,\n      });\n      const orbitLine = new THREE.Line(orbitLineGeom, orbitLineMat);\n      orbitLine.visible = settingsRef.current.showOrbits;\n      scene.add(orbitLine);\n      orbitLinesList.push(orbitLine);\n\n      planetsList.push({\n        id: pData.id,\n        data: pData,\n        pivot: orbitPivot,\n        mesh: pMesh,\n        currentOrbitAngle: initialAngle,\n        orbitDistance: pData.distance,\n        orbitSpeed: pData.orbitSpeed,\n        rotationSpeed: pData.rotationSpeed,\n        ringMesh,\n      });\n    });\n\n    planetMeshesRef.current = planetsList;\n    orbitLinesRef.current = orbitLinesList;\n\n    // 8. レンダリングループ\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n    const pointer = new THREE.Vector2();\n\n    const animate = () => {\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n      const speed = currentSettings.isPaused ? 0 : currentSettings.speedMultiplier;\n\n      // 太陽自転\n      if (sunMeshRef.current) {\n        sunMeshRef.current.rotation.y += SUN_DATA.rotationSpeed * speed;\n      }\n\n      // 惑星公転・自転\n      planetMeshesRef.current.forEach((item) => {\n        item.currentOrbitAngle += item.orbitSpeed * 0.4 * speed;\n        item.mesh.position.x = Math.cos(item.currentOrbitAngle) * item.orbitDistance;\n        item.mesh.position.z = Math.sin(item.currentOrbitAngle) * item.orbitDistance;\n        item.mesh.rotation.y += item.rotationSpeed * speed;\n      });\n\n      // カメラ追従処理\n      const focusedId = currentSettings.focusedPlanetId;\n      let targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n      let targetCameraDistance = 140;\n\n      if (focusedId) {\n        if (focusedId === 'sun') {\n          targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n          targetCameraDistance = 30;\n        } else {\n          const focusedPlanet = planetMeshesRef.current.find((p) => p.id === focusedId);\n          if (focusedPlanet) {\n            targetPos.copy(focusedPlanet.mesh.position);\n            targetCameraDistance = Math.max(focusedPlanet.data.radius * 7, 10);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // 目標座標へカメラターゲットを滑らかに移動 (lerp)\n      cameraTargetRef.current.lerp(targetPos, 0.05);\n\n      // 球面座標からカメラ位置を計算\n      const { theta, phi, radius } = cameraAnglesRef.current;\n      // 選択中は適正距離に補正\n      if (focusedId) {\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(radius, targetCameraDistance, 0.05);\n      }\n\n      const camX = cameraTargetRef.current.x + cameraAnglesRef.current.radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      const camY = cameraTargetRef.current.y + cameraAnglesRef.current.radius * Math.cos(phi);\n      const camZ = cameraTargetRef.current.z + cameraAnglesRef.current.radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n\n      camera.position.set(camX, camY, camZ);\n      camera.lookAt(cameraTargetRef.current);\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n\n    animate();\n\n    // 9. ポインター／ドラッグ操作イベント\n    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      isDraggingRef.current = true;\n      previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n      pointerDownPosRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!isDraggingRef.current) return;\n\n      const deltaX = e.clientX - previousPointerPositionRef.current.x;\n      const deltaY = e.clientY - previousPointerPositionRef.current.y;\n\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.theta -= deltaX * 0.006;\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.phi -= deltaY * 0.006;\n\n      // 垂直角度の制限（真上・真下でのフリップ防止）\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.phi = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.1, cameraAnglesRef.current.phi));\n\n      previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      isDraggingRef.current = false;\n\n      // クリック判定（移動量が小さい場合のみ選択）\n      const dx = Math.abs(e.clientX - pointerDownPosRef.current.x);\n      const dy = Math.abs(e.clientY - pointerDownPosRef.current.y);\n      if (dx < 6 && dy < 6) {\n        const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();\n        pointer.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n        pointer.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n        raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);\n        const clickableMeshes: THREE.Object3D[] = [];\n        if (sunMeshRef.current) clickableMeshes.push(sunMeshRef.current);\n        planetMeshesRef.current.forEach((p) => clickableMeshes.push(p.mesh));\n\n        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(clickableMeshes, false);\n        if (intersects.length > 0) {\n          const hitMesh = intersects[0].object;\n          const hitId = hitMesh.userData.id;\n          if (hitId) {\n            onSelectPlanet(hitId);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      const zoomFactor = e.deltaY > 0 ? 1.1 : 0.9;\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = Math.max(8, Math.min(600, cameraAnglesRef.current.radius * zoomFactor));\n    };\n\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container || !renderer || !camera) return;\n      const w = container.clientWidth;\n      const h = container.clientHeight;\n      camera.aspect = w / h;\n      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      renderer.setSize(w, h);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    const canvasEl = renderer.domElement;\n    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n    canvasEl.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      canvasEl.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n      canvasEl.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);\n\n      scene.clear();\n      renderer.dispose();\n      starGeometry.dispose();\n      starMaterial.dispose();\n    };\n  }, [onSelectPlanet]);\n\n  return <div ref={containerRef} className=\"canvas-container\" />;\n};\n\nexport default SolarSystemCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

orbitLinesRef.current.forEach((line) => {\n      line.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n    });\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(300, 900);\n      const theta = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0, Math.PI * 2);\n      const phi = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0, Math.PI);\n\n      starPositions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n\n      const tint = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0.7, 1.0);\n      starColors[i * 3] = tint;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = tint * 0.95;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = tint * 1.1;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new scene.add(starField);\n\n    // 5. 環境光と光源（太陽光）\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.22);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff7ed, tiltGroup.position.set(\n        Math.cos(initialAngle) * pData.distance,\n        0,\n        Math.sin(initialAngle) * pData.distance\n      );\n      orbitPivot.add(tiltGroup);\n\n      // 軌道線（輪郭）\n      const curve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(\n        0, 0,\n        pData.distance, pData.distance,\n        0, 2 * Math.PI,\n        false,\n        0\n      );\n      const points = item.currentOrbitAngle += item.orbitSpeed * 0.4 * speed;\n        item.tiltGroup.position.x = Math.cos(item.currentOrbitAngle) * item.orbitDistance;\n        item.tiltGroup.position.z = Math.sin(item.currentOrbitAngle) * item.orbitDistance;\n        // 自転軸に沿った回転\n        item.mesh.rotation.y += item.rotationSpeed * speed;\n      });\n\n      // カメラ追従処理\n      const focusedId = currentSettings.focusedPlanetId;\n      let targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n      let targetCameraDistance = DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES.radius;\n\n      if (focusedId) {\n        if (focusedId === 'sun') {\n          targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n          targetCameraDistance = 30;\n        } else {\n          const focusedPlanet = planetMeshesRef.current.find((p) => p.id === focusedId);\n          if (focusedPlanet) {\n            targetPos.copy(focusedPlanet.tiltGroup.position);\n            targetCameraDistance = Math.max(focusedPlanet.data.radius * 7, 10);\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        // 未選択時は全体俯瞰角度へスムーズにリセット誘導\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.theta = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(\n          cameraAnglesRef.current.theta,\n          DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES.theta,\n          0.04\n        );\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.phi = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(\n          cameraAnglesRef.current.phi,\n          DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES.phi,\n          0.04\n        );\n      }\n\n      // 目標座標へカメラターゲットを滑らかに移動 (lerp)\n      cameraTargetRef.current.lerp(targetPos, 0.05);\n\n      // 距離を滑らかに補正\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.radius,\n        targetCameraDistance,\n        0.05\n      );\n\n      // 球面座標からカメラ位置を計算\n      const { theta, phi, radius } = cameraAnglesRef.current;\n      const camX = cameraTargetRef.current.x + radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      const camY = cameraTargetRef.current.y + radius * Math.cos(phi);\n      const camZ = cameraTargetRef.current.z + radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n\n      camera.position.set(camX, camY, camZ);\n      camera.lookAt(cameraTargetRef.current);\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n\n    animate();\n\n    // 9. ポインター／ドラッグ操作イベント\n    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      activeTouchesRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });\n\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 1) {\n        isDraggingRef.current = true;\n        previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n        pointerDownPosRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n      } else if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 2) {\n        // 2点タッチピンチ開始\n        isDraggingRef.current = false;\n        const points = Array.from(activeTouchesRef.current.values());\n        initialPinchDistanceRef.current = Math.hypot(\n          points[0].x - points[1].x,\n          points[0].y - points[1].y\n        );\n        initialRadiusRef.current = cameraAnglesRef.current.radius;\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.has(e.pointerId)) {\n        activeTouchesRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });\n      }\n\n      // 2点タッチピンチズーム処理\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 2 && initialPinchDistanceRef.current !== null) {\n        const points = Array.from(activeTouchesRef.current.values());\n        const currentDistance = Math.hypot(\n          points[0].x - points[1].x,\n          points[0].y - points[1].y\n        );\n        if (currentDistance > 0) {\n          const ratio = initialPinchDistanceRef.current / currentDistance;\n          cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = Math.max(8, Math.min(600, initialRadiusRef.current * ratio));\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 1点ドラッグによる回転\n      if (!isDraggingRef.current) return;\n\n      const deltaX = e.clientX - previousPointerPositionRef.current.x;\n      const deltaY = e.clientY - previousPointerPositionRef.current.y;\n\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.theta -= deltaX * 0.006;\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.phi -= deltaY * 0.006;\n\n      // 垂直角度の制限（真上・真下でのフリップ防止）\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.phi = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.1, cameraAnglesRef.current.phi));\n\n      previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      activeTouchesRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size < 2) {\n        initialPinchDistanceRef.current = null;\n      }\n\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 1) {\n        const remainingPoint = Array.from(activeTouchesRef.current.values())[0];\n        previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: remainingPoint.x, y: remainingPoint.y };\n        isDraggingRef.current = true;\n      } else {\n        isDraggingRef.current = false;\n      }\n\n      // クリック判定（移動量が小さい場合のみ選択）\n      const dx = Math.abs(e.clientX - pointerDownPosRef.current.x);\n      const dy = Math.abs(e.clientY - pointerDownPosRef.current.y);\n      if (dx < 6 && dy < 6 && activeTouchesRef.current.size === 0) {\n        const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();\n        pointer.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n        pointer.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n        raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);\n        const clickableMeshes: THREE.Object3D[] = [];\n        if (sunMeshRef.current) clickableMeshes.push(sunMeshRef.current);\n        planetMeshesRef.current.forEach((p) => clickableMeshes.push(p.mesh));\n\n        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(clickableMeshes, false);\n        if (intersects.length > 0) {\n          const hitMesh = intersects[0].object;\n          const hitId = hitMesh.userData?.id;\n          if (hitId) {\n            onSelectPlanet(hitId);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      const zoomFactor = e.deltaY > 0 ? 1.1 : 0.9;\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = Math.max(8, Math.min(600, cameraAnglesRef.current.radius * zoomFactor));\n    };\n\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container || !renderer || !camera) return;\n      const w = container.clientWidth;\n      const h = container.clientHeight;\n      camera.aspect = w / h;\n      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      renderer.setSize(w, h);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    const canvasEl = renderer.domElement;\n    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n    canvasEl.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      canvasEl.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n      canvasEl.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);\n\n      // 太陽リソース破棄\n      if (sunMeshRef.current) {\n        sunMeshRef.current.geometry.dispose();\n        if (Array.isArray(sunMeshRef.current.material)) {\n          sunMeshRef.current.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n        } else {\n          sunMeshRef.current.material.dispose();\n        }\n      }\n      if (coronaMeshRef.current) {\n        coronaMeshRef.current.geometry.dispose();\n        if (Array.isArray(coronaMeshRef.current.material)) {\n          coronaMeshRef.current.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n        } else {\n          coronaMeshRef.current.material.dispose();\n        }\n      }\n\n      // 各惑星メッシュおよびリングリソース破棄\n      planetsList.forEach((p) => {\n        p.mesh.geometry.dispose();\n        if (Array.isArray(p.mesh.material)) {\n          p.mesh.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n        } else {\n          p.mesh.material.dispose();\n        }\n\n        if (p.ringMesh) {\n          p.ringMesh.geometry.dispose();\n          if (Array.isArray(p.ringMesh.material)) {\n            p.ringMesh.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n          } else {\n            p.ringMesh.material.dispose();\n          }\n        }\n      });\n\n      // 軌道線リソース破棄\n      orbitLinesList.forEach((line) => {\n        line.geometry.dispose();\n        if (Array.isArray(line.material)) {\n          line.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n        } else {\n          line.material.dispose();\n        }\n      });\n\n      // 背景星屑破棄\n      starGeometry.dispose();\n      starMaterial.dispose();\n\n      scene.clear();\n      renderer.dispose();\n    };\n  }, [onSelectPlanet]);\n\n  return <div ref={containerRef} className=\"canvas-container\" />;\n};\n\nexport default SolarSystemCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ControlsBar.tsx","content":"import { ChangeEvent } from 'react';\nimport { SimulationSettings } from '../types';\nimport { SUN_DATA, PLANETS_DATA } from '../solarData';\n\ninterface ControlsBarProps {\n  settings: SimulationSettings;\n  onUpdateSettings: (updater: (prev: SimulationSettings) => SimulationSettings) => void;\n  onSelectPlanet: (id: string | null) => void;\n  onOpenModal: () => void;\n}\n\nconst ControlsBar = ({\n  settings,\n  onUpdateSettings,\n  onSelectPlanet,\n  onOpenModal,\n}: ControlsBarProps) => {\n  const allCelestialBodies = [SUN_DATA, ...PLANETS_DATA];\n\n  const handleTogglePause = () => {\n    onUpdateSettings((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      isPaused: !prev.isPaused,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  const handleSpeedChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);\n    onUpdateSettings((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      speedMultiplier: isNaN(val) ? 1.0 : val,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  // 速度を1.0x（等倍）にリセット\n  const handleResetSpeed = () => {\n    onUpdateSettings((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      speedMultiplier: 1.0,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  const handleToggleOrbits = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const checked = e.target.checked;\n    onUpdateSettings((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      showOrbits: checked,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  // 全体俯瞰へリセット（追従解除＆カメラ位置リセット誘導）\n  const handleResetCamera = () => {\n    onSelectPlanet(null);\n  };\n\n  const selectedBody = allCelestialBodies.find((b) => b.id === settings.focusedPlanetId);\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {/* 惑星クイックセレクターバー */}\n      <div className=\"planet-selector-bar\">\n        {allCelestialBodies.map((body) => {\n          const isActive = settings.focusedPlanetId === body.id;\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={body.id}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={isActive ? data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport './index.css';\nimport { PlanetData, SimulationSettings } from './types';\nimport { SUN_DATA, PLANETS_DATA } from './solarData';\nimport SolarSystemCanvas from './components/SolarSystemCanvas';\nimport ControlsBar from './components/ControlsBar';\nimport PlanetInfoModal from './components/PlanetInfoModal';\n\n// 全体俯瞰（未選択時）用の太陽系全体データ\nconst SOLAR_SYSTEM_DATA: PlanetData = {\n  id: 'solar-system',\n  name: '太陽系 全体',\n  englishName: 'Solar System',\n  type: 'star',\n  radius: 0,\n  distance: 0,\n  distanceAU: '約 100,000 AU (オールトの雲)',\n  orbitSpeed: 0,\n  rotationSpeed: 0,\n  axialTilt: 60.19, // 銀河面に対する傾斜角\n  gravity: '支配天体: 太陽',\n  color: '#38bdf8',\n  overview: '太陽を中心とし、水星から海王星までの8つの惑星、小惑星帯、エッジワース・カイパーベルト、オールトの雲で構成される惑星系。約46億年前に巨大分子雲の重力崩壊によって誕生し、天の川銀河のオリオン腕を秒速約230kmで公転しています。',\n  diameterKm: '約 2875億 km (カイパーベルト外縁)',\n  orbitPeriodDays: '約 2億3000万年 (銀河系公転周期)',\n  surfaceTemp: '平均 2.7 K (-270.5 ℃ / 宇宙背景放射)',\n  moonsCount: 293,\n  notableMoons: ['月', 'エウロパ', 'ガニメデ', 'タイタン', 'エンケラドゥス', 'トリトン'],\n};\n\nconst ALL_BODIES: PlanetData[] = [SUN_DATA, ...PLANETS_DATA];\n\nconst App = () => {\n  // シミュレーション設定の初期状態\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<SimulationSettings>({\n    speedMultiplier: 1.0,\n    isPaused: false,\n    showOrbits: true,\n    focusedPlanetId: 'earth', // 初期状態で地球にフォーカス\n  });\n\n  // 惑星詳細モーダルの表示状態\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 惑星選択ハンドラー (同一天体クリック時は詳細モーダルを開く)\n  const handleSelectPlanet = useCallback((id: string | null) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => {\n      // 既に選択されている天体が再度クリックされた場合は詳細モーダルを開く\n      if (id !== null && prev.focusedPlanetId === id) {\n        setIsModalOpen(true);\n      }\n      return {\n        ...prev,\n        focusedPlanetId: id,\n      };\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  // 3D空間ダブルクリックでモーダルを開く\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleDoubleClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      // モーダル内やHUDコントロール上のクリックでなければ発火\n      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;\n      if (target.closest('.controls-panel') || target.closest('.top-header') || target.closest('.info-card')) {\n        return;\n      }\n      setIsModalOpen(true);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('dblclick', handleDoubleClick);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('dblclick', handleDoubleClick);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // キーボードショートカット (Space: 再生/停止, 左右矢印: 天体切替, 数字キー: 天体ダイレクト選択, Iキー: モーダル開閉, Cキー: 俯瞰リセット)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      // フォーム入力中のショートカット無効化\n      const activeTag = (document.activeElement?.tagName || '').toLowerCase();\n      if (activeTag === 'input' || activeTag === 'textarea' || activeTag === 'select') {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Spaceキー: 再生 / 一時停止切替\n      if (e.code === 'Space') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setSettings((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          isPaused: !prev.isPaused,\n        }));\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 'i' / 'I' キー: 詳細モーダル開閉\n      if (e.key === 'i' || e.key === 'I') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setIsModalOpen((prev) => !prev);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 'c' / 'C' キー: 全体俯瞰へリセット\n      if (e.key === 'c' || e.key === 'C') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setSettings((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          focusedPlanetId: null,\n        }));\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 数字キー 0〜8: 天体ジャンプ (0: 太陽, 1: 水星, ..., 8: 海王星)\n      const num = parseInt(e.key, 10);\n      if (!isNaN(num) && num >= 0 && num < ALL_BODIES.length) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setSettings((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          focusedPlanetId: ALL_BODIES[num].id,\n        }));\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 左右矢印キー: 天体順送り/逆送り\n      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setSettings((prev) => {\n          const currentIndex = prev.focusedPlanetId\n            ? ALL_BODIES.findIndex((b) => b.id === prev.focusedPlanetId)\n            : -1;\n\n          let nextIndex = 0;\n          if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n            nextIndex = currentIndex === -1 ? 0 : (currentIndex + 1) % ALL_BODIES.length;\n          } else {\n            nextIndex = currentIndex <= 0 ? ALL_BODIES.length - 1 : currentIndex - 1;\n          }\n\n          return {\n            ...prev,\n            focusedPlanetId: ALL_BODIES[nextIndex].id,\n          };\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // 選択された天体データの取得 (未選択時は太陽系全体の概要データをフォールバック)\n  const focusedPlanet =\n    ALL_BODIES.find((b) => b.id === settings.focusedPlanetId) || SOLAR_SYSTEM_DATA;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"solar-app\">\n      {/* 3D レンダリングキャンバス */}\n      <SolarSystemCanvas\n        settings={settings}\n        onSelectPlanet={handleSelectPlanet}\n      />\n\n      {/* HUD オーバーレイ UI */}\n      <div className=\"hud-overlay\">\n        {/* 上部ヘッダー */}\n        <header className=\"top-header\">\n          <h1 className=\"app-title\">\n            <span>☀️ リアルタイム3D太陽系シミュレーター</span>\n          </h1>\n          <p className=\"app-subtitle\">\n            ドラッグで視点回転・ホイール/ピンチでズーム・クリック/数字キーで天体選択・ダブルクリック/Iキーで解説\n          </p>\n        </header>\n\n        {/* コントロール群 (惑星バー & 下部操作パネル) */}\n        <ControlsBar\n          settings={settings}\n          onUpdateSettings={setSettings}\n          onSelectPlanet={handleSelectPlanet}\n          onOpenModal={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 惑星・太陽および太陽系全体の詳細解説モーダル */}\n      <PlanetInfoModal\n        planet={focusedPlanet}\n        isOpen={isModalOpen}\n        onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

SimulationSettings } from '../types';\nimport { SUN_DATA, PLANETS_DATA } from '../solarData';\n\ninterface SolarSystemCanvasProps {\n  settings: SimulationSettings;\n  onSelectPlanet: (id: string | null) => void;\n}\n\ninterface PlanetMeshInfo {\n  id: string;\n  data: PlanetData;\n  pivot: THREE.Group;\n  tiltGroup: THREE.Group;\n  mesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  currentOrbitAngle: number;\n  orbitDistance: number;\n  orbitSpeed: number;\n  rotationSpeed: number;\n  ringMesh?: THREE.Mesh;\n}\n\n// 惑星の自転軸の傾斜（ラジアン）\nconst PLANET_TILTS: Record<string, number> = {\n  mercury: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(0.034),\n  venus: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(177.3),\n  earth: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(23.44),\n  mars: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(25.19),\n  jupiter: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(3.13),\n  saturn: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(26.73),\n  uranus: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(97.77),\n  neptune: THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(28.32),\n};\n\nconst DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES = {\n  theta: Math.PI / 4,\n  phi: Math.PI / 3,\n  radius: 140,\n};\n\nconst SolarSystemCanvas = ({\n  settings,\n  onSelectPlanet,\n}: SolarSystemCanvasProps) => {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  // Three.js インスタンス保持用のRef\n  const sceneRef = useRef<THREE.Scene | null>(null);\n  const cameraRef = useRef<THREE.PerspectiveCamera | null>(null);\n  const rendererRef = useRef<THREE.WebGLRenderer | null>(null);\n  const planetMeshesRef = useRef<PlanetMeshInfo[]>([]);\n  const sunMeshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh | null>(null);\n  const coronaMeshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh | null>(null);\n  const orbitLinesRef = useRef<THREE.Line[]>([]);\n\n  // 操作とカメラ遷移制御\n  const isDraggingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);\n  const previousPointerPositionRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n  const cameraAnglesRef = useRef<{ theta: number; phi: number; radius: number }>({\n    ...DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES,\n  });\n  const cameraTargetRef = useRef<THREE.Vector3>(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0));\n  const pointerDownPosRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number }>({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n\n  // タッチ操作管理 (ピンチズーム対応)\n  const activeTouchesRef = useRef<Map<number, { x: number; y: number }>>(new Map());\n  const initialPinchDistanceRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const initialRadiusRef = useRef<number>(140);\n\n  // settingsの最新状態をアニメーションループ内で参照\n  const settingsRef = useRef<SimulationSettings>(settings);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n    // 軌道線表示切替\n    orbitLinesRef.current.forEach((line) => {\n      line.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n    });\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!container) return;\n\n    const width = container.clientWidth;\n    const height = container.clientHeight;\n\n    // 1. シーン初期化\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    sceneRef.current = scene;\n\n    // 2. カメラ初期化\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, width / height, 0.1, 2000);\n    cameraRef.current = camera;\n\n    // 3. レンダラー初期化\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({\n      antialias: true,\n      alpha: true,\n      powerPreference: 'high-performance',\n    });\n    renderer.setSize(width, height);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\n    container.innerHTML = '';\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n    rendererRef.current = renderer;\n\n    // 4. 背景の星屑パーティクル\n    const starsCount = 2000;\n    const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    const starPositions = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n    const starColors = new Float32Array(starsCount * 3);\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < starsCount; i++) {\n      const radius = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(300, 900);\n      const theta = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0, Math.PI * 2);\n      const phi = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0, Math.PI);\n\n      starPositions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n\n      const tint = THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(0.7, 1.0);\n      starColors[i * 3] = tint;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = tint * 0.95;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = tint * 1.1;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      size: 1.5,\n      vertexColors: true,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.85,\n    });\n    const starField = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);\n    scene.add(starField);\n\n    // 5. 環境光と光源（太陽光）\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.22);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff7ed, 3.2, 500, 0.5);\n    sunLight.position.set(0, 0, 0);\n    scene.add(sunLight);\n\n    // 6. 太陽の生成\n    const sunGeom = new THREE.SphereGeometry(SUN_DATA.radius, 32, 32);\n    const sunMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n      color: SUN_DATA.color,\n    });\n    const sunMesh = new THREE.Mesh(sunGeom, sunMat);\n    sunMesh.userData = { id: SUN_DATA.id };\n    scene.add(sunMesh);\n color: 0xff6600,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.25,\n      side: THREE.BackSide,\n    });\n    const coronaMesh = new THREE.Mesh(coronaGeom, coronaMat);\n    sunMesh.add(coronaMesh);\n    coronaMeshRef.current = coronaMesh;\n\n    // 7. 惑星および軌道線の生成\n    const planetsList: PlanetMeshInfo[] = [];\n    const orbitLinesList: THREE.Line[] = [];\n\n    PLANETS_DATA.forEach((pData, index) => {\n      // 軌道グループピボット\n      const orbitPivot = new THREE.Group();\n      scene.add(orbitPivot);\n\n      // 自転軸傾斜（tilt）用のラッパーグループ\n      const tiltGroup = new THREE.Group();\n      const tiltAngle = PLANET_TILTS[pData.id] || 0;\n      tiltGroup.rotation.z = tiltAngle;\n\n      // 惑星本体\n      const pGeom = new THREE.SphereGeometry(pData.radius, 32, 32);\n      const pMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n        color: pData.color,\n        roughness: 0.6,\n        metalness: 0.2,\n      });\n      const pMesh = new THREE.Mesh(pGeom, pMat);\n      pMesh.userData = { id: pData.id };\n      tiltGroup.add(pMesh);\n\n      // 土星などの環（リング）\n      let ringMesh: THREE.Mesh | undefined;\n      if (pData.rings) {\n        const ringGeom = new THREE.RingGeometry(pData.rings.innerRadius, pData.rings.outerRadius, 64);\n        const ringMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: pData.rings.color,\n          side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.8,\n          roughness: 0.5,\n        });\n        ringMesh = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeom, ringMat);\n        ringMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; // 赤道面に水平に配置（tiltGroupで一緒に傾く）\n        tiltGroup.add(ringMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 初期の公転位置を散らして自然に見せる\n      const initialAngle = (index / PLANETS_DATA.length) * Math.PI * 2;\n      tiltGroup.position.set(\n        Math.cos(initialAngle) * pData.distance,\n        0,\n        Math.sin(initialAngle) * pData.distance\n      );\n      orbitPivot.add(tiltGroup);\n\n      // 軌道線（輪郭）\n      const curve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(\n        0, 0,\n        pData.distance, pData.distance,\n        0, 2 * Math.PI,\n        false,\n        0\n      );\n      const points = curve.getPoints(128);\n      const orbitLineGeom = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(\n        points.map((pt) => new THREE.Vector3(pt.x, 0, pt.y))\n      );\n      const orbitLineMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n        color: 0x38bdf8,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: 0.3,\n      });\n      const orbitLine = new THREE.Line(orbitLineGeom, orbitLineMat);\n      orbitLine.visible = settingsRef.current.showOrbits;\n      scene.add(orbitLine);\n      orbitLinesList.push(orbitLine);\n\n      planetsList.push({\n        id: pData.id,\n        data: pData,\n rotationSpeed: pData.rotationSpeed,\n        ringMesh,\n      });\n    });\n\n    planetMeshesRef.current = planetsList;\n    orbitLinesRef.current = orbitLinesList;\n\n    // 8. レンダリングループ\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n    const pointer = new THREE.Vector2();\n\n    const animate = () => {\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n      const speed = currentSettings.isPaused ? 0 : currentSettings.speedMultiplier;\n\n      // 太陽自転\n      if (sunMeshRef.current) {\n        sunMeshRef.current.rotation.y += SUN_DATA.rotationSpeed * speed;\n      }\n\n      // 惑星公転・自転\n      planetMeshesRef.current.forEach((item) => {\n        item.currentOrbitAngle += item.orbitSpeed * 0.4 * speed;\n        item.tiltGroup.position.x = Math.cos(item.currentOrbitAngle) * item.orbitDistance;\n        item.tiltGroup.position.z = currentSettings.focusedPlanetId;\n      let targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n      let targetCameraDistance = DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES.radius;\n\n      if (focusedId) {\n        if (focusedId === 'sun') {\n          targetPos = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n          targetCameraDistance = 30;\n        } else {\n          const focusedPlanet = planetMeshesRef.current.find((p) => p.id === focusedId);\n          if (focusedPlanet) {\n            targetPos.copy(focusedPlanet.tiltGroup.position);\n            targetCameraDistance = Math.max(focusedPlanet.data.radius * 7, 10);\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        // 未選択時は全体俯瞰角度へスムーズにリセット誘導\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.theta = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(\n          cameraAnglesRef.current.theta,\n          DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES.theta,\n          0.04\n        );\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.phi = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(\n          cameraAnglesRef.current.phi,\n          DEFAULT_CAMERA_ANGLES.phi,\n          0.04\n        );\n      }\n\n      // 目標座標へカメラターゲットを滑らかに移動 (lerp)\n      cameraTargetRef.current.lerp(targetPos, 0.05);\n\n      // 距離を滑らかに補正\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(\n        cameraAnglesRef.current.radius,\n        targetCameraDistance,\n        0.05\n      );\n\n      // 球面座標からカメラ位置を計算\n      const { theta, phi, radius } = cameraAnglesRef.current;\n      const camX = cameraTargetRef.current.x + radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      const camY = cameraTargetRef.current.y + radius * Math.cos(phi);\n      const camZ = cameraTargetRef.current.z + radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n\n      camera.position.set(camX, camY, camZ);\n      camera.lookAt(cameraTargetRef.current);\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n\n    animate();\n\n    // 9. ポインター／ドラッグ操作イベント\n    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      activeTouchesRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });\n\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 1) {\n        isDraggingRef.current = true;\n        previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n        pointerDownPosRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n      } else if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 2) {\n        // 2点タッチピンチ開始\n        isDraggingRef.current = false;\n        const       initialRadiusRef.current = cameraAnglesRef.current.radius;\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.has(e.pointerId)) {\n        activeTouchesRef.current.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });\n      }\n\n      // 2点タッチピンチズーム処理\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 2 && initialPinchDistanceRef.current !== null) {\n        const points = Array.from(activeTouchesRef.current.values());\n        const currentDistance = Math.hypot(\n          points[0].x - points[1].x,\n          points[0].y - points[1].y\n        );\n        if (currentDistance > 0) {\n          const ratio = initialPinchDistanceRef.current / currentDistance;\n          cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = Math.max(8, Math.min(600, initialRadiusRef.current * ratio));\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 1点ドラッグによる回転\n      if (!isDraggingRef.current) return;\n\n      const deltaX = e.clientX - previousPointerPositionRef.current.x;\n      const deltaY = e.clientY - previousPointerPositionRef.current.y;\n\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.theta -= deltaX * 0.006;\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.phi -= deltaY * 0.006;\n\n      // 垂直角度の制限（真上・真下でのフリップ防止）\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.phi = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.1, cameraAnglesRef.current.phi));\n\n      previousPointerPositionRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      activeTouchesRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size < 2) {\n        initialPinchDistanceRef.current = null;\n      }\n\n      if (activeTouchesRef.current.size === 1) {\n        const remainingPoint =      isDraggingRef.current = false;\n      }\n\n      // クリック判定（移動量が小さい場合のみ選択）\n      const dx = Math.abs(e.clientX - pointerDownPosRef.current.x);\n      const dy = Math.abs(e.clientY - pointerDownPosRef.current.y);\n      if (dx < 6 && dy < 6 && activeTouchesRef.current.size === 0) {\n        const rect = renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect();\n        pointer.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n        pointer.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n        raycaster.setFromCamera(pointer, camera);\n        const clickableMeshes: THREE.Object3D[] = [];\n        if (sunMeshRef.current) clickableMeshes.push(sunMeshRef.current);\n        planetMeshesRef.current.forEach((p) => clickableMeshes.push(p.mesh));\n\n        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(clickableMeshes, false);\n        if (intersects.length > 0) {\n          const hitMesh = intersects[0].object;\n          const hitId = hitMesh.userData?.id;\n          if (hitId) {\n            onSelectPlanet(hitId);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      const zoomFactor = e.deltaY > 0 ? 1.1 : 0.9;\n      cameraAnglesRef.current.radius = Math.max(8, Math.min(600, cameraAnglesRef.current.radius * zoomFactor));\n    };\n\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container || !renderer || !camera) return;\n      const w = container.clientWidth;\n      const h = container.clientHeight;\n      camera.aspect = w / h;\n      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      renderer.setSize(w, h);\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n    const canvasEl = renderer.domElement;\n    canvasEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n    canvasEl.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      canvasEl.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n      canvasEl.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);\n\n      // 太陽リソース破棄\n      if (sunMeshRef.current) {\n        sunMeshRef.current.geometry.dispose();\n        if (Array.isArray(sunMeshRef.current.material)) {\n          sunMeshRef.current.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n        } else {\n          sunMeshRef.current.material.dispose();\n        }\n      }\n      if (coronaMeshRef.current) {\n    coronaMeshRef.current.material.dispose();\n        }\n      }\n\n      // 各惑星メッシュおよびリングリソース破棄\n      planetsList.forEach((p) => {\n        p.mesh.geometry.dispose();\n        if p.ringMesh.material.dispose();\n          }\n        }\n      });\n\n      // 軌道線リソース破棄\n      orbitLinesList.forEach((line) => {\n        line.geometry.dispose();\n        if ...prev,\n      isPaused: !prev.isPaused,\n    }));\n  };\n\n  const handleSpeedChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);\n const closeButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null);\n  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  // Escapeキーで閉じる処理 & フォーカス管理 & スクロールロック\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!isOpen) return;\n\n    // 初回フォーカスを閉じるボタンへ当てる\n    const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n      closeButtonRef.current?.focus();\n    }, 50);\n\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        onClose();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Tabキーでのフォーカストラップ\n      if (e.key === 'Tab' && modalRef.current) {\n        const focusableElements = modalRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\n          'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\"-1\"])'\n        );\n        if (focusableElements.length === 0) return;\n\n        const firstElement = focusableElements[0];\n        const lastElement = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1];\n\n        if (e.shiftKey) setSettings((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          isPaused: !prev.isPaused,\n        }));\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 'i' / 'I' キー: 詳細モーダル開閉\n      if (e.key === 'i' || e.key === 'I') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setIsModalOpen((prev) => !prev);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // 'c' / 'C' キー: 全体俯瞰へリセット\n      if (e.key === 'c' || e.key === 'C') {\n e.preventDefault();\n        setSettings((prev) => {\n          const currentIndex = prev.focusedPlanetId\n            ? ALL_BODIES.findIndex((b) => b.id === prev.focusedPlanetId)\n            : -1;\n\n          let nextIndex = 0;\n          if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {\n            nextIndex = currentIndex === -1 ? 0 : (currentIndex + 1) % ALL_BODIES.length;\n          } else {\n            nextIndex = currentIndex <= 0 ? ALL_BODIES.length - 1 : currentIndex - 1;\n          }\n\n          return {\n            ...prev,\n            focusedPlanetId: ALL_BODIES[nextIndex].id,\n          };\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // 選択された天体データの取得
